## Done this session — P7 / P8 fitting renderer

- Read GDD, ROADMAP, REVIEW and PROGRESS first. REVIEW's five fixes were already implemented; retained crate/reward differentiation, compact headings/navigation, independent multi-material checks and actual delivery flights. Full browser regression covers the delivery behaviors and screenshots preserve the compact layouts.
- `js/shoe-renderer.js`: studio and worn source artwork now share a **24 MiB LRU cache** (RGBA source pixels, excluding transient composition buffers and displayed canvases). Previously studio alone could retain 32 × 1000×660 sources (~80.6 MiB), while the same worn design was recomposed separately for both feet and the reflection. Worn cache hits now reuse the exact source and old designs are evicted by actual pixel cost.
- Canvas hydration remembers each element's design, rendering mode and dimensions in a WeakMap. Unchanged notifications preserve existing backing stores. Layout width, rather than the transformed screen rectangle, sets resolution, so rotating/zooming does not inflate allocations. Device pixel ratio remains capped at 2; actual orientation/layout changes still redraw correctly.
- `js/app.js`: coalesce window/ResizeObserver callbacks into one animation frame. Apply fitting/Boutique modal classes **before** initial layout/hydration, and hydrate after the introduction changes its layout. This fixes undersized first-open fitting canvases and restores correct sizing on return paths.
- `style.css`: move foot brightness/shadow filters onto the static canvas inside the moving foot layer, preserving the art, soft shadows and pose/sway controls while allowing the transformed parent to reuse its raster.
- Added `qa/renderer-test.py` and `qa/renderer-report.json`: all five sizes verify final first-open resolution, three poses, zero repeated width/height allocations, zoom, real resize/portrait rotation, DPR cap, worn-source identity, studio/worn key separation, LRU eviction and Collection → another fitting return. README documents this command and the cache budget.
- Rebuilt runtime archive: **156 files / 5.38 MiB**. No new runtime assets, font changes or network dependencies.
